    WF-15 — Study v0 on the lossy sphere¶

    Item briefs and execution history (operator directive 2026-10-01). The §7 row is the status of record.

    §9 item 240 — 2026-10-05 (chain U3)¶

    Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-05 03:00 daily review; the review reviews it. Licence: §10 chain U3 (PROJECT_PLAN.md:4622–4625), a weekly-enumerated step (§9 rule (4)); WF-14 ✅ 2026-10-04 (20261005T022839Z_WF-14-W1.log) meets "serial on U2". No band, tolerance, Done-when or validation target moves.

    WF-15 step 1: Study v0 on MAT-4 step 1's lossy sphere. One scriptable object composes five things: a geometry generator, a MAT-1 material, an imposed field, a solve (degree 2 by default) and a SAR result. Assembled this way, it must reproduce MAT-4 step 1's mean-SAR figure against the direct call, and land inside MAT-4's band, at -n 2. Its public names are then registered in WF-14's envelope. - Executor: implementer. New src/ module plus one additive closed-form helper and the __all__ export; a new test module; the registry and snapshot. No existing gate module, helper or record is edited. - Tier: W0 / W1 standard, W2 heavy (re-declared from the row's "standard by prediction" by pricing below), W3 / W4 the WF-14 / WF-13 windows verbatim. -n 2 throughout, which is the row's letter. Complex build for every solve window. - Class: chain step (solve), measured median ≈ 16 min (2026-10-04 18:00 restock). - Cost: ≈ 16 measured / ≈ 60 predicted min. That is 15 fixed + ≈ 25 code + windows ≈ 1 + 2 + 7 + 0.5 + 1 min + ≈ 8 spare. Tight against the 1-hour timebox, and W2 is most of the compute. - Depends on: nothing open. WF-14's files are on main, and nothing named Study exists in src/ (grep, 2026-10-05).

    Settled from the code (read before designing). - MAT-4 step 1's figure is degree 1, and there is no degree-2 record of it. The gate solves at TimeHarmonicSolver(problem, degree=1) (tests/validation/test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:189). The fine rung is h = R/10 in / R/5 out. On the 0.11 image it reads 8.291558e-08 W/kg, 3.536 % at σ = 0.57 S/m on 74 020 cells (20260921T111802Z_OPS-55-callers-complex.log.gz:1137). The same rung reads 8.291558128e-08 to ten digits on the material_map path (20260930T003229Z_MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:494). The 2026-08-03 figure, 8.291696e-08 on 74 019 cells (20260803T020448Z_MAT-4-step1-gate.log.gz:499), is a pre-0.11-image record and cannot be reproduced today. No module solves this fixture at degree 2. test_lossy_sphere_degree2.py (TH-12) is the TH-10 Larmor sphere, a different fixture. Do not invent a degree-2 MAT-4 record. - How "degree 2 by default" squares with "digit-identical against the direct call". The comparand is the direct call at the Study's own degree, on the same mesh. MAT-4's band is the closed-form gate at either degree. So the item has two legs: - the default leg (degree 2): the Study equals the direct degree-2 call, and both sit inside MAT-4's band; - the record leg (degree=1 passed explicitly): the Study reproduces the degree-1 figure above, which is the only figure MAT-4 step 1 owns. - The direct call is MAT-1's _solve_case material_map branch (tests/materials/test_phantom_material_model.py:360–407), copied into the new module with a degree parameter. The source is hard-coded degree=1 at :383 and is not edited. At degree 1 the copy must reproduce :494's map SAR, which proves the copy faithful ((D1) below). - The material slot is MAT-1's class, GelledSalinePhantomMaterial (src/fem_em_solver/materials/phantom.py:22). It takes either a preset name (from_preset, :43; presets low / mid / high = (0.45, 80) / (0.75, 78) / (1.05, 76), :13–18) or an explicit instance. - MAT-4's σ = 0.57, εᵣ = 78 is not a preset. The figure legs pass GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(0.57, 78.0, 64e6). MAT-1's (P1) / (P2) showed this assembles bitwise the material_map path's arrays and operator (…MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:485–490). - The preset-by-name route is exercised by a "mid" leg, which reproduces MAT-1's own record (:497). - It goes in as phantom_material with phantom_tag=1. The default 3 raises on this mesh (core/time_harmonic.py:113, :278–282). - The imposed field has no src/ home. The quasi-static exterior (uniform + dipole) field exists only as the test-private _exact_exterior_numpy (test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:98–124). An installed package cannot import tests/, which is WF-13's rule. - TimeHarmonicSolver's default degree is 1 (core/time_harmonic.py:318). The Study must pass its own.

    Code. - (1) src/fem_em_solver/utils/analytical.py: new dielectric_sphere_quasistatic_field(*, radius, epsilon_c, e0=1.0). It returns the (3, n) callable. - Its arithmetic is copied verbatim, in the same operation order, from test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:104–122: beta = (epsilon_c - 1.0) / (epsilon_c + 2.0); e_in = 3.0 * e0 / (epsilon_c + 2.0); common = beta * e0 * R**3 / r_safe**5; and so on. It returns dtype=PETSc.ScalarType. - It is additive: no existing function changes. - (2) New src/fem_em_solver/study.py with three public names, all frozen dataclasses or a plain class: - SphereInBoxGeometry(sphere_radius, box_half_width, resolution_sphere, resolution_far, sphere_tag=1). Its .build(comm) wraps MeshGenerator.sphere_in_box_domain (io/mesh.py:2545). - QuasiStaticSphereField(e0=1.0). It builds the Dirichlet callable through (1), from the geometry's radius and ε_c = complex(epsilon_r, -sigma / (omega * EPSILON_0)). Here ω = 2.0 * np.pi * frequency_hz, the same expression as test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:79 and phantom.py:122. - Study(geometry, material, excitation, *, frequency_hz, degree=2, density_kg_m3=1000.0). material is a GelledSalinePhantomMaterial or a preset name. Background is HomogeneousMaterial(sigma=0.0, epsilon_r=1.0), and the boundary is "pec_zero_tangential_a" with dirichlet_e_field. - .solve(comm=MPI.COMM_WORLD) builds the mesh and returns a result exposing mesh, cell_tags, fields, solver and degree. - .mean_sar() calls post.sar.mean_sar(fields.e_complex, sigma=fields.sigma_field, rho=density_kg_m3, cell_tags=…, subdomain_ids=sphere_tag, comm=…) and returns its dict unchanged. - No import from tests/, and no new solver code: the Study only composes existing calls. - (3) src/fem_em_solver/__init__.py: export the three names and add them to __all__ (25 → 28). - (4) tests/package/public_surface.txt: add the three names, sorted. tests/package/validation_envelope.toml: one [names.X] table per new name, each listing the WF-15 gate nodes below. Node IDs only, no number (WF-14 (N)). - (5) New tests/package/test_wf15_study_lossy_sphere.py, @complex_only on every solve test (tests/complex_mode.py:26), so that WF-14's real-build collection imports it cleanly. - Imports (names only, nothing restated): SPHERE_RADIUS, BOX_HALF_WIDTH, E0, EPSILON_R_SPHERE, FREQUENCY_HZ, OMEGA, RHO_KG_M3, SIGMA_HIGH, SPHERE_TAG, _exact_exterior_numpy and _interior_probe_points from test_lossy_sphere_sar.py. From test_phantom_material_model.py: MAT4_BAND (:222), SOLVE_FLOOR_RECORD (:239), _dg0_owned_diff, _same_pattern, _difference, _volume_operator (:246–329) and build_preset_sphere_mesh (:434). From test_mat5_em_coupled_sphere.py:104: L0_BAND. - Fixtures: module-scoped deg1 and deg2. Each builds the Study, solves it, and then runs the direct call on the Study's own mesh / cell_tags, so the upstream compares share one owned layout. - Nodes: - test_wf15_imposed_field_is_mat4_wall_data_bitwise (no solve); - test_wf15_study_default_degree_is_two (no solve); - test_wf15_degree1_reproduces_mat4_record; - test_wf15_preset_leg_reproduces_mat1_record; - test_wf15_default_degree2_matches_direct_call.

    Anchors (asserted). - (F) The imposed field is MAT-4's wall data, bitwise. Evaluate QuasiStaticSphereField at σ = 0.57 / εᵣ 78 and _exact_exterior_numpy(SIGMA_HIGH) on _interior_probe_points(), the box corners and the rank's owned geometry nodes. Assert np.array_equal. Predicted exact: same floats, same operation order. - (Deg) The default is degree 2, structurally. Study(...).degree == 2 without the argument, and the solved e_complex space's element degree == 2. Both are exact. - (M) The geometry generator equals the direct generator. The global cell count of SphereInBoxGeometry(R, 0.02, R/10, R/5).build equals build_preset_sphere_mesh(comm)'s in the same process, exactly. It is printed beside the version-tagged 74 020 (…MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:485), never asserted at equality to that number. - (U) The upstream identity, bitwise, at the leg's degree. This is MAT-1's (P1) / (P2) construction, Study against direct: - DG0 σ / εᵣ / μᵣ: max |Δ| == 0.0 with 0 differing on owned dofs, and owned total == global cells; - the curl-curl operators from each path's fields have the same CSR pattern and ‖A_study − A_direct‖_∞ == 0.0. - Asserted at degree 1 (W1) and degree 2 (W2). - (P) SAR post-processing is bitwise. The Study's mean_sar_w_per_kg == mean_sar(...) called directly on the Study's own fields (same field, same process, a deterministic assembly plus a 2-rank allreduce). - (D1) The direct copy is faithful (degree 1). The direct SAR against :494's map record 8.291558128e-08, relative ≤ L0_BAND 1e-9. - (I) The Study equals the direct call end to end, at the leg's degree. |SARtudy − SAR_direct| / SAR_rect ≤ L0_BAND and |⟨E⟩_study − ⟨E_z⟩_dirt| / |⟨Ez⟩_direct ≤ L0_BAND, printed at .10e. - Why this and not bitwise:** two MUMPS solves at -n 2 are not bitse-identical even in one process. The floor on this fixture is 5.962e-13 … 4.450e-11 (known-issues.md:81; SOLVE_FLOOR_RECORD). - Why L0_BAND:** it is this fixture's existing in-run lve-to-solve band. MAT-5 step 2's (L0) measured 7.193e-11 against it (20260930T124701Z_MAT-5-step2-W2.log:160), so it sits ≈ 22× above the floor. - Asserted at degree 1. At degree 2 it is asserted on the imported band. That the degree-2 floor lies under it is redicted* (unmeasured), and the measured value printed. - (R) MAT-4's figure, digit-identical (record leg, degree=1). - (R1) The Study's SAR against 8.291558128e-08, relative ≤ L0_BAND. - (R2) f"{sar:.6e}" == "8.291558e-08", which is MAT-4's own print format (test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:303) and its 0.11-image figure (…OPS-55…:1137). The value …558128 is 3.7e-7 relative from the .6e rounding edge, against a 4.5e-11 floor, so the string compare is safe. - (G) Inside MAT-4's imported band, at both degrees. - Mean SAR against uniform_sphere_sar_closed_form(e0=E0, epsilon_r=78, sigma=0.57, omega=OMEGA, rho=RHO_KG_M3, epsilon_0=EPSILON_0) ≤ MAT4_BAND 0.10 (test_lossy_sphere_sar.py:321, bound at test_phantom_material_model.py:222). - Interior Im/Re E_z ≤ 0.10 (:347), through evaluate_vector_field_parallel. - The interior spread < 0.02 (:331, a literal bound by value with that citation, as MAT-1 did). - (Pre) The preset route ("mid", degree=1). - Study(material="mid")'s SAR printed .4e equals MAT-1's 6.8832e-08 (…MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:497). - Its error against the closed form is ≤ MAT4_BAND (3.548 % recorded).

    Printed (predicted, not asserted). - The degree-2 closed-form error. Predicted in [0.2, 4] %. The arithmetic: MAT-4's own rungs read 8.679 % at R/6 and 3.536 % at R/10 (…OPS-55…:1136–1137). An O(h²) fit puts the degree-1 limit at ≈ 0.6 %. Degree 2 removes most of the h-error but not the faceted-sphere volume (V_mesh/V_exact 0.996387) or the O((k_in R)²) model term (|k_in|R = 0.179). - The degree-2 Im/Re error. Predicted ≤ 0.6 % (degree 1: 0.55 %). - SAR_d2 / SAR_d1 − 1. Predicted in [−3.5, 0] %. - Degree-2 DOFs. Predicted ≈ 497 k (6.72 per cell, as IMP-1's rungs). - The degree-2 SAR at .10e. This becomes WF-15's record for WF-17. - Per-solve seconds and summed peak ru_maxrss (never cgroup memory.peak, TH-12 caution).

    Negative controls (rule (e)). - (c1) 1-ULP σ through the Study's material path. Asserted. - The planted perturbation is GelledSalinePhantomMaterial(np.nextafter(0.57, 1), 78, f) through build_material_fields(..., phantom_tag=1). No solve is needed. - The check: (U)'s array compare against the direct σ must read > 0 on > 0 dofs. - Backing: MAT-1 (c1) measured exactly 2⁻⁵³ = 1.1102230246251565e-16 on 19 496 dofs (…MAT-1-reanchor-W1.log:491), the same comparison on the same fixture. DG0 arrays do not depend on the N1curl degree. - Ceiling: 2⁻⁵³ is the smallest nonzero reading the compare can return, so this control shows that (U)'s == 0.0 is at full resolution. - (c2) σ·(1 + 1e-9) on the operator. - At degree 1, asserted: ‖A″ − A_direct‖_∞ > 0, backed by 1.1777e-9 (relative 2.41e-14, :493). - At degree 2, predicted O(1e-9). It is printed, and its > 0 is not asserted. - (c3) The wrong material end to end, (I)'s comparator turned on a known mismatch (degree 1). Asserted. - The check: |SARid − SAR_direct(0.57)| / SAR_direct >klzzwxh:0271. - Ceiling: the closed-form separation is |6.6474e-08 / 8.008354e-08 − 1| = 16.99 %. The measured value on this fixture is 16.98 % (:494 vs :497), ≈ 1.7e8 × the band. - This shows that (I) can fail. - (c4) Dege mismatch (W2). Predicted. - (I)'s comrator, Study (degree 2) against a direct degree-1 solve (one extra ≈ 12 s solve), must exceed L0_BAND. Predicted 0.1–3.5 %; it is printed only, because no degree-2 measurement exists on this fixture. - (Deg) carries the asserted half. - Not re-bought: MAT-4's two-σ σ-blind control (separation 4.850 against ceiling 4.855, > 3). It stays MAT-4's, at degree 1. The record leg reproduces that gate's figure, and W0 re-runs it.

    Traps already paid for. - No solve output is ever compared bitwise. - MAT-1 item 81 went red at 1e-12 on an identical re-run (known-issues.md:81). - MAT-5 item 237's (R0c) went red at 2.747e-12 against an unbacked 1e-12 band in one process (20261005T050759Z_MAT-5-step3-W1-alpha0.log:1874–1882), today. - Bitwise belongs upstream of the solve only: (F), (U), (P). - Operation order is part of bitwise. ω must be 2.0 * np.pi * f, ε_c must be complex(εᵣ, -σ / (ω ε₀)), and the field arithmetic must copy :104–122. A rearranged product fails (F) by an ULP, and it is meant to. - phantom_tag default 3 raises on this mesh. A tag set in both material_map and phantom_material raises too (core/time_harmonic.py:262). The direct path uses material_map only. - TimeHarmonicSolver(problem) silently means degree 1 (:318). (Deg) is there to catch a fall-through. - Rank-safety. - DG0 compares use the owned slice [:size_local] with reductions (_dg0_owned_diff). - _difference only runs after _same_pattern is True on every rank (its collective guard, :301–303). - cell_tags.values is rank-local. - Points go through evaluate_vector_field_parallel. - The src/ package never imports tests/. WF-13's installed venv has no /workspace. W4 catches it. - WF-14 (N): the registry holds node IDs only. The snapshot is sorted. Adding to __all__ without both edits makes W3 red by design. - complex_only on solve tests, or WF-14's real-build collection errors out (rc 2). - Orphan .c: sweep only with pgrep -c python3 = 0. A killed degree-2 compile poisons its cache entry. - Memory: price by summed ru_maxrss, never cgroup memory.peak (TH-12 step 1 caution). - No pipe or redirect in the harness command (the footer records the pipe's status).     Findings at drafting (source only, not run). 1. Study.probe cannot reach a thermal-only temperature. _study_probe calls self.temperature(cooling) with no transient (src/fem_em_solver/study.py:1539). On a ThermalBallGeometry that dispatches to _study_transient_temperature (:1253–1254), which raises TypeError when transient is None (:1446–1447), before any mesh. The fix is a keyword pass-through, not a new algorithm: ball_average (thermal/probe.py:168–171) works on any P1 θ. 2. MAT-19's record cannot be reproduced to its digits on a ball Study. MAT-19's (T) 1.7315e-03 is a maximum over 3 probes × 40 times on MAT-12's EM-solved sphere-in-box tag-1 submesh, 19 496 cells (20261005T095808Z_MAT-19-step1-W1.log.gz:203; tests/validation/test_mat19_virtual_temperature_probe.py:83). A ThermalBallGeometry is a gmsh ball_domain (study.py:1343–1348), a different mesh, and Study.probe returns one reading per probe at the end time, not a series (ProbeStudyResult, :1509–1519). - What can be reproduced is MAT-19's gate: its exact Helmholtz ball-mean comparand _ball_mean (test_mat19_…py:117–127) and its band, at t* on the same physics (R, k, h, ρc, q_cf, probes). - Item 326 scoped exactly this out: "Not claimed: … MAT-19's (T) 1 % gate (it is on the q_cf transient, not reproduced here)" (this file, :392). This item closes that. - A time series through the Study (snapshots) is not posed. 3. The band name. MAT-19's band is P_BAND = 1e-2 (tests/validation/test_mat10_sar_to_heat_sphere.py:59, imported at test_mat19_…py:56). U60 says INTERIOR_BAND, which is also 1e-2 but is MAT-13's constant, the one MAT-21 uses. The drafter imports each half's own constant: P_BAND for the ball (MAT-19's gate) and INTERIOR_BAND for the box (MAT-21's). The values are equal and no band moves (question 2). 4. "Point probes" is read as probes centred at points. Study.probe is MAT-19's ball average (an owned-cell integral). Scalar point evaluation on the box is P37's (MAT-21 step 10, PROJECT_PLAN.md:4808–4816) and is not claimed. 5. The box comparand at a probe is exact. On the steady box with uniform q, ∇²θ = −Q/K, so θ + Q|x − c|²/(6K) is harmonic. The ball mean over B(c, a) is therefore θ̄ = θ_series(c) − Q a²/(10K) whenever the ball lies inside the box (mean of |x − c|² over the ball = 3a²/5). This is the identity MAT-19's _ball_mean steady term carries for the sphere (steady … − 3a²/5, test_mat19_…py:126). The truncated series obeys it to its truncation deficit, 4.6708e-6 (COMPARAND_FLOOR, tests/validation/test_mat21_box_fem_p2.py:83). 6. The three helpers (study.py:155–168) on a thermal-only Study, predicted: - omega → TypeError (2.0 * np.pi * None); - epsilon_c() → AttributeError (None.epsilon_r); - dirichlet_field() → AttributeError ('UniformHeatSource' object has no attribute 'dirichlet_field'). W0 observes them before any edit (item 507's pattern). 7. StudyConfigError lives in study_yaml.py (src/fem_em_solver/study_yaml.py:158–165, a ValueError subclass with (source, field, reason)), and study_yaml imports study at module level. So study.py can raise it only through a function-local import, which is cycle-safe at call time. Every sibling thermal-only refusal raises TypeError (:171–172, :533–537, :1446–1447), so the letter's StudyConfigError makes these three a ValueError among TypeErrors (question 1).

    For the review. 1. StudyConfigError from the Python API (the letter), through a function-local import, gives a ValueError beside sibling TypeError refusals. The alternative is TypeError naming the field in the message, which is consistent but not the letter. 2. Each half imports its own 1 % constant (P_BAND for MAT-19, INTERIOR_BAND for MAT-21). The values are equal. 3. "Reproduce MAT-19's record" is read as its gate (comparand + band at t*), not its digits, which belong to a different mesh and a 40-time series. A series through the Study (snapshot pass-through) would be a further step. 4. The box half's dependency is 521a only, not 521b. The item runs its ball half and helpers regardless.

    Review note 2026-11-05 18:00 — item 831 BLOCKED, left to the 11-07 weekly (not re-posed)¶

    • The red is the brief's own named negative, so its unblock is the weekly's ("the weekly re-poses the band"). This review does not re-pose it under rule (6): both candidate re-poses move what (S2-tip) compares against — a wider band, or a mass-consistent comparand — and rule (6) never moves a band or a target.
    • The brief's premise is contradicted by the measurement. It read (S2-tip) red as "the conduction term". At Δt₁/10 the top-tip deficit moves only 4.3368e-03 → 4.2166e-03 and the bottom 5.9425e-04 → 6.8113e-04, while (S2) scales 4.8353e-06 → 4.8365e-07 (20261105T212316Z_WF-15-s14-W1-r3.log). A conduction term would scale with Δt. The executor's reading: the first step's 1 g ball mean of a P1 θ under the consistent mass is a mass-matrix-weighted mean of the DG0 q, not the ball's own q̄ — a Δt-independent difference largest where q varies most (the top tip). For the weekly: (a) re-pose (S2-tip)'s band, or (b) assert the tip slope against the mass-consistent mean (Δt·mean(M⁻¹b)/ρc on the ball) at S2_BAND with q̄ printed beside — (b) keeps the band and changes the comparand.
    • Disclosure carried: W1 crashed twice in non-gate code before any gate value (a src running_average on a one-step series; a test-side facet-tag lookup) and was fixed and re-run twice; the 10-31 weekly's narrow ruling allows one. Nothing closes on those logs (the item is parked); the weekly may rule whether the second re-run stands.
